---

## HOW TO USE THIS LIST

1. At the start of every session: read this file + `HUGR_Master_Inventory_v1_0.md` + `HUGR_Master_Implementation_Spec.md`
2. Pick the next uncompleted task from the priority queue
3. Do the work
4. Mark the task complete with `[x]` and add the commit hash
5. Update `HUGR_Master_Inventory_v1_0.md` with the new feature status
6. Commit both files to GitHub before ending the session

### Local Watch54w first-draw seam source root cause — 2026-09-17

**ROOT CAUSE — correction specified, not implemented.** The exact first-frame seam registers `ViewTreeObserver.OnDrawListener`, then removes the listener from inside its own `onDraw()` callback before it writes `FIRST_DRAW_OBSERVED` or posts deferred startup. Android’s public API prohibits adding/removing an `OnDrawListener` from that callback; Android 14 framework source throws `IllegalStateException` for this operation when target SDK is Android O or later. The Watch54w candidate targets SDK 34. This deterministically explains the observed last durable stage (`FIRST_FRAME_PRESENTATION_SET`) and absent next stage; the same self-removing seam is present in the pre-breadcrumb first-frame integration copy. The frozen smallest correction is to defer removal until after draw dispatch returns while retaining the same listener, on-draw evidence boundary, and deferred-startup ordering. No code, build, artifact, device action or launcher/exporter change is authorized by this result. See `docs/HUGR_NSTAIR_R0_2_Watch54w_First_Draw_Seam_Source_Root_Cause_2026_09_17.md`.

### Evidence Egress v1 — joined source/test integration 2026-09-21

**SOURCE-LEVEL JOINED PROTOCOL / TEST GREEN — PHYSICAL INTEROPERABILITY NOT ESTABLISHED.** The Termux/ADB route is formally stopped as the current extraction method; it remains a development/recovery fallback rather than a recurring product or N1 workflow. The Watch now has four dedicated pull-style egress characteristics and the phone now uses their actual UUIDs, parses the Watch’s compact `E1/v1` offer/chunk/control/receipt frames, stages opaque bytes only in phone-private storage, resumes ordered chunks, checks P1 frozen identity binding before staging, writes the final receipt only after `PHONE_COPY_IDENTICAL`, and rejects a mismatched Watch receipt transfer ID. The compact offer is MTU-128-safe (119 bytes); chunks are at most 118 bytes and the final receipt is 99 bytes. Live telemetry, source controls, journal/scientific memory, cloud/portal, sensing, haptics and the exact-range generator remain separate.

**PHONE53 POST-MTU / FIRST-OFFER-READ SEAM — SOURCE/TEST DIAGNOSIS COMPLETE / NO ROOT CAUSE ESTABLISHED / NO CORRECTION OR RETRY.** The authorised source-only diagnosis has eliminated the concrete suspected Phone helper defect: in installed `react-native-ble-plx 3.5.1`, discovery may return a new JavaScript `Device` wrapper, but discovery, service lookup and characteristic reads all delegate by the same stable device ID to one Android native active-connection map. - [ ] **EDI-7 — Define collaborator-grade reproducible export**
  - Include consent/access scope, data dictionary, hashes, immutable manifest, analysis environment/version, missingness/null/failure evidence and an independent reproduction test without exposing unauthorized PHI.

  - Current state: **VISION/SPECIFIED/BLOCKED**. Requires EDI-1–7, privacy/consent, redaction, query/audit provenance, citation/abstention tests and no action, diagnosis, haptic, ledger-rewrite or claim-promotion authority.

It creates no fifth gate and does not delay the separately authorized decision about one Revision 0.2 Gate 1 retry.

Default honestly to `PHONE_CANONICAL_ONLY`/`PHONE_ONLY_PENDING_PACKAGE`; allow explicit `NONE`. Do not make Neil transcribe engineering fields.

  - At the ordinary END anchor, derive coverage, charging/off-wrist/unknown intervals, lineages, replay/ACK/recovery, duplicates/collisions, gaps, quality/abstention and simple human anchors from existing evidence. This is part of Gate 2 PASS/QUALIFIED PASS, not another gate.

  - Under separate Gate 3 authority, issue a scoped N1 credential, verify exact event/batch ACK, retry/idempotency and protected replay, register raw artifact identities, regenerate one completeness table and one time-series figure, then trace one non-pristine window back to canonical watch identities. Complete EDI-2/6/7 only to the minimum proven Golden Path; do not build a general evidence platform.

  - Register every prediction’s frozen feature/model bundle and training cutoff. Before any learning claim, ensure each accepted update can reconstruct the before/after model state or append checksummed model snapshots; never back-project a later model into earlier predictions.

  - Correct “all data local/no cloud” copy; distinguish local canonical/raw evidence, automatic coarse derived upload, protected scientific-memory events and explicit clinician sharing. Authenticate/retire public legacy ingestion, define access/retention/deletion/restore ownership and test the chosen external package route. This is pre-external-pilot work, not a current named-pair gate.

### Canonical Human Context Bridge (`CHC-BRIDGE`)

> Canonical specification: `docs/HUGR_Canonical_Human_Context_Bridge_and_Exposure_Annotation_Preservation_2026_08_31.md`. This is general context infrastructure, not a medication feature: human/context event → canonical identity/time/provenance → qualified physiology → versioned analysis/knowledge state. It does not change Gate 1, tomorrow’s Gate 2 burden or the gate count.

- [x] **CHC-0 — Audit existing context/exposure preservation and identify the missing joint**
  - Local `LogEvent`/`RetroLog`, broad medication/substance concepts and the canonical scientific-memory `Context` envelope exist. The local-event→canonical-event→qualified-physiology bridge is missing. Narrow payload gaps: medication dose+unit+taken-at, nicotine route/form and structured meditation timing.
- [ ] **CHC-1 — Freeze general context types and payload profiles before formal multi-day N1 instrument freeze**
  - Preserve local event ID, event time versus entry time, source/certainty, retrospective delay, effective interval, clock uncertainty, original free text and context-specific value/unit/route. Medication, caffeine, nicotine, other exposure, meditation, sleep, craving/urge and clinical reports use the same bridge.
- [ ] **CHC-2 — Adapt new local events into canonical scientific-memory `Context` envelopes**
  - Preserve the original local payload and identity; add participant/session/source instance/sequence, build/schema, quality/confounds/missingness and event hash; use exact idempotent retry. Do not relabel historical local rows as already canonical.
- [ ] **CHC-3 — Link context events to exact qualified physiology windows**
  - Derived windows must reference both canonical `Context` events and exact watch-record/source ranges or qualified epoch identities; preserve clock correction/uncertainty, gaps, charging/off-wrist, quality, replay, exclusions, transformations and eligibility.
- [ ] **CHC-4 — Prove one context→physiology reverse trace inside existing Gate 3**
  - Include context events and linked windows in the checksummed Evidence Package; verify protected-ledger round trip and reproduce one context-aligned output back to canonical watch identities. This is part of MEL-3/4, not a fifth gate.
- [ ] **CHC-5 — Preserve analysis knowledge state**
  - Every context-linked inference/prediction stores model/rule/config version, training or knowledge cutoff, input cutoff and evidence identities. Later reanalysis appends a new result and never back-projects later knowledge.
- [ ] **CHC-6 — Add low-burden capture/UI only after contract and adapter freeze**
  - Implement payload-specific fields where scientifically justified. Do not add them to the current named-pair Gate 2 rehearsal or make Neil technical transcription infrastructure.
- [ ] **CHC-D1 — Exposure-pattern detection study — LATER / HYPOTHESIS**
  - Test association/classification around labeled exposures only with adequate structured events, qualified physiology, timing uncertainty and confound-aware analysis. No urge or causal claim.
- [ ] **CHC-D2 — Prospective physiology-before-reported-urge study — DREAM-PARKED**
  - Requires silent frozen predictions, explicit urge event/report time, optional noticed-onset time, fixed horizons, comparators and prospective scoring. Report time is not awareness onset.
- [ ] **CHC-D3 — Causal interpretation — SEPARATE CONTROLLED DESIGN ONLY**
  - Exposure timestamps or predictive trajectories alone cannot establish that exposure or physiology caused an urge/state change.

### Proven non-cardiac blockers before broad N=1

  - A visible `0%` or other composite score must identify which streams/freshness/contact conditions produced it.

  - `Wrist: unknown` cannot be presented as a live validated sensor conclusion when the updater is not wired.

  - Include delayed callback batches, reconnects, phone restart and mixed-freshness streams.

  - Exact Build 44 was remotely preserved and device-tested. ACCEL delivery and background continuity failed; recovery moved to separately specified Build 45 rather than being hidden or patched ad hoc.

## P0 — MUST BE IN NEXT BUILD (before Kari pilot)

> **2026-07-17 update**: Five critical wiring items added. These are not features — they are schema and architecture decisions that cannot be deferred without losing data that can never be recovered retroactively. The Kari pilot generates the first real dataset. Every check-in stored without these fields is a permanently missed data point.

- [ ] **P0.NEW-1 — Context tagging at check-in** *(UNLOCKS Relational Dysregulation Index biomarker)*
  - Add 5 tappable chips below the sliders: Alone / Partner / Work / Social / Client
  - Optional, not required — but encouraged with a brief prompt
  - Schema: add `context: 'alone' | 'partner' | 'work' | 'social' | 'client' | null` to `CheckIn` interface in `lib/storage.ts`
  - Without this, the RDI hypothesis cannot be tested on Kari pilot data
  - Effort: Small (2–3 hours)

- [ ] **P0.NEW-2 — Fusion estimate stored at check-in** *(UNLOCKS correct Symbiosis Score)*
  - Store `fusionEstimate: { state: StateKey, confidence: number, weights: object }` alongside each check-in record
  - Currently the fusion estimate is computed but not stored — Symbiosis Score cannot be correctly computed without it
  - Schema: add `fusionEstimate` to `CheckIn` interface in `lib/storage.ts`
  - Effort: Small (1–2 hours)

- [ ] **P0.NEW-3 — Data record stamping** *(CRITICAL for research data integrity)*
  - Every `CheckIn` record must include: `connectedDevices: DeviceId[]`, `signalQuality: 'high' | 'medium' | 'low'`, `fusionConfidence: number`
  - Without this, Tier 1 data cannot be distinguished from Tier 3 data in the research dataset
  - Schema: add metadata fields to `CheckIn` interface per `HUGR_Signal_Architecture_Addendum_2026_07_08.md` §5.3
  - Effort: Small (1–2 hours)

- [ ] **P0.NEW-4 — Honest labels on Arc Time and Symbiosis Score** *(integrity)*
  - Add info buttons (ⓘ) to Arc Time and Symbiosis Score on home screen and clinician screen
  - Arc Time info: "Currently estimated from time between consecutive check-ins. Precision increases with HRV (Tier 3)."
  - Symbiosis Score info: "Currently measures agreement between time-of-day/motion estimate and self-report. Precision increases with HRV and EDA."
  - Effort: Small (1 hour)

- [ ] **P0.NEW-5 — Data tier badge on home screen** *(integrity)*
  - Persistent "Tier 1 · Phone only" badge in home screen header
  - Tappable — opens a brief explanation of what Tier 1 means and what higher tiers add
  - Effort: Small (1 hour)

## P0 — ORIGINAL ITEMS (all completed)

> **2026-07-08 update**: The P0 list below reflects the original task list (commit `31690bd`). A later diagnosis session (2026-07-08) identified four additional P0 bugs in the code. Three have been fixed in commit `516ae2a`; one was verified as already correct.

### Original P0 items (from commit `31690bd` era)

- [x] **P0-orig-1 — Remove `regulate.tsx` tab** — VERIFIED ALREADY DONE
  - `regulate.tsx` is absent from `app/(tabs)/`. No change needed.

- [x] **P0-orig-2 — Fix “Antidote” language in user-facing copy** — VERIFIED ALREADY CLEAN
  - All remaining “antidote” occurrences are in developer comments only.
  - `settings.tsx` has zero occurrences. No user-facing string contains the word.

- [x] **P0-orig-3 — Check-in button not working** — VERIFIED CORRECT (no change needed)
  - `router.push('/(tabs)/checkin')` at home.tsx line 746 is correct.
  - `checkin` is registered as a hidden route (`href: null`) in `_layout.tsx`.
  - Issue was likely a build-specific regression in `f80214a7`, not a code bug.

- [x] **P0-orig-4 — Haptic screen content below Symbiosis Score not scrollable** — FIXED commit `d6f1b01`
  - Root cause: Skia `Canvas` `pointerEvents="none"` not fully respected on some Android builds.
  - Fix: wrapped `Canvas` in a `View` with `pointerEvents="none"` at the React Native layer.
  - `View` establishes a proper touch-interception boundary that Android respects unconditionally.

### Diagnosed P0 bugs (2026-07-08 session) — fixed in commit `516ae2a`

- [x] **P0.1 — Home orb does not update after check-in** — FIXED commit `516ae2a`
  - Root cause: `runFusion()` called concurrently with `loadData()` in `useFocusEffect`; read stale `checkIns[0]` from component state.
  - Fix: `loadData()` now returns fresh check-ins; `useFocusEffect` awaits it before calling `runFusion(fresh)`; `runFusion()` accepts optional `freshCheckIns` param.

- [x] **P0.2 — BioParticles crash/freeze** — FIXED commit `516ae2a`
  - Root cause: `Skia.Paint()` allocated inside `createPicture` per-particle per-frame (64 native allocs/frame at 60fps); `Skia.MaskFilter.MakeBlur()` also allocated per-particle per-frame.
  - Fix: `sharedPaint` created once via `useMemo`; `blurCache` Map caches `SkMaskFilter` objects by quantised blur value.

- [x] **P0.3 — Save button hangs with no visual feedback** — VERIFIED CORRECT (no change needed)
  - `finally` block at checkin.tsx line 250 always runs; inner fusion `try/catch` prevents escape; motion snapshot has 1s timeout guard.

- [x] **P0.4 — Haptics cannot be stopped once started (iOS)** — FIXED commit `516ae2a`
  - Root cause: iOS `while (Date.now() < endTime)` loop had no cancellation mechanism; `cancel()` only cleared Android timer.
  - Fix: module-level `hapticCancelled` flag; `play()` resets it; `cancel()` sets it; `playOnce()` checks it after every `await`; while-loop checks it at each iteration.

## P1 — MUST HAVE BEFORE SHOWING TO FRIENDS OR CLINICIAN

- [x] **P1.1 — HUGR rune + wordmark persistent on EVERY screen**
  - Currently only on Home screen
  - Prototype: rune ᛞ + "HUGR" wordmark appear in the same top-left position on every screen, making all screens feel like glass sliding over the same bioluminescent night sky
  - Implementation: create a shared `HUGRHeader` component with the animated rune + wordmark; import it into every screen's header area
  - Exact rune SVG: see `LANDING_DESIGN_VALUES.md` (7 lines + centre circle, stroke #5db87a, rotate 90°)
  - Exact wordmark: DM Sans, 15.2px, weight 400, letter-spacing 0.12em, glow animation 3s cycle
  - Effort: 2–3 hours

- [x] **P1.2 — BioParticles quality — 3-layer architecture matching prototype**
  - User reports: particles not as smooth as web demo; needs more particles with much higher velocity; some should dart across screen
  - Current: BioParticles v3 uses simple sine drift, 81/40 particles, size 0.8–10.5px
  - Target: 3-layer architecture from prototype — 28 background (slow, small, dim) + 20 midground (medium) + 16 foreground (fast, large, bright); 25% fireflies with flash behaviour; 10% fast-movers that dart
  - Reference: `PROTOTYPE_SOURCE_EXTRACTION.md` §1–4 for exact particle parameters per layer
  - Architecture stays as declarative Skia Circle/BlurMask/Group + RAF loop (BioParticles v3 is correct foundation)
  - Effort: 3–4 hours

- [x] **P1.2b — BioParticles depth blur — restore bokeh depth illusion from web demo**
  - User confirmed: current particles look flat; demo had a strong sense of depth because background particles were heavily blurred (out of focus)
  - Fix: differentiate blur radius strongly by layer:
    - Background layer (28 particles): blur radius 6–8px — heavily out of focus, small, dim
    - Midground layer (20 particles): blur radius 2–3px — slightly soft
    - Foreground layer (16 particles): blur radius 0–1px — sharp, bright, largest
  - The `blurCache` Map in BioParticles v4 already caches MaskFilter objects by blur value — this is a parameter change, not an architecture change
  - Also: background particles should be significantly dimmer (opacity 0.15–0.3) vs foreground (opacity 0.7–1.0) to reinforce the depth illusion
  - Can be done in the same session as P1.2 (same file, same loop)
  - Effort: 30–45 minutes (parameter tuning only)

- [x] **P1.4 — Fix check-in sliders smooth**

  - Check `checkin.tsx`: verify slider onValueChange uses the debounce correctly; may need to switch to a custom slider implementation with native driver

- [x] **P1.5 — Fix breath animation screen AppState restore**

## BUILD 25 — EXPANDED SCOPE (Phone BLE + Circadian + Full Sensor Architecture)

> **Date:** 2026-08-03 (Desktop day)
> **Canonical Reference:** HUGR_Full_Sensor_Clinical_Architecture_v1.md + BUILD_25_WEAROS_BLE_INTEGRATION.md
> **Principle:** Build the data model for the full sensor suite from day one, even if not all sensors are active yet. This prevents architectural debt.

### Build 25 Core (Must complete on desktop day)

- [ ] **B25.1 — BLE GATT client on phone app**
  - Connects to watch's BLE GATT server
  - Receives EDA, IBI, accelerometer streams
  - Validates packet integrity
  - Stores raw data locally
  - Effort: 3-4 hours

- [ ] **B25.2 — Full sensor data model (future-proof schema)**
  - All fields from HUGR_Full_Sensor_Clinical_Architecture_v1.md Section 1
  - Circadian fields: sleep onset/offset, stages, efficiency, SpO2, skin temp
  - Medication event logging structure
  - Clinician intervention rule data structure
  - Confidence score fields on every state estimate
  - Effort: 2 hours

- [ ] **B25.3 — Activity context gating (noise disambiguation)**
  - Static/Dynamic/Transition detection from accelerometer
  - Motion-correction applied to EDA/HRV during dynamic context
  - 3-5 minute cooldown after movement before clinical readings
  - Cross-validation matrix (EDA + HRV + Accel agreement)
  - Reference: Architecture doc Section 3
  - Effort: 2-3 hours

- [ ] **B25.4 — Confidence score computation**
  - SensorAgreement (0.4) + CircadianConsistency (0.2) + HistoricalPattern (0.2) + SymbiosisAlignment (0.2)
  - Every state estimate carries a confidence score
  - States < 0.6 confidence shown with uncertainty band
  - States > 0.85 shown as high-confidence clinical data
  - Effort: 1-2 hours

- [ ] **B25.5 — Live signal feed display (Signal/Text mode)**
  - Scrolling EDA waveform (last 60 min)
  - Scrolling HRV waveform (last 60 min)
  - Movement context indicator
  - Sleep summary from last night
  - Circadian phase indicator
  - Confidence score display
  - Effort: 3-4 hours

## STRATEGY AND RESEARCH TRACK (added 2026-07-08)

These are not code tasks. They are the strategic actions that make the app fundable, credible, and acquirable.

- [ ] **S0 — Register Oura OAuth2 app (WHEN RING ACQUIRED)**
  - Oura account requires Oura Ring + active subscription (€5.99/month) — no developer-only account path exists
  - **Workaround confirmed 2026-07-09:** Oura sandbox API works with no account, no ring, no real token
    - Sandbox verified live: returns real-shaped data for all endpoints

### P1.11 Roots tree — what was fixed (2026-07-09)
- SEG_H increased from 7.5px → 26px (28 days now fills ~728px — visible on device)
- WOBBLE increased from 12px → 26px (organic lateral movement)
- Glow layer opacity increased from 0.18 → 0.32, strokeWidth from 3.2x → 5x thickness
- Segment thickness increased from 4.5px → 6.5px at root
- Tendril density increased (fires on ~75% of completed segments, not every 2nd/3rd)
- Tendril length increased from 28–66px → 44–100px
- Tendril strokeWidth increased from 1.8px → 2.4px
- Glow dots at tendril base/tip enlarged (r 1.6→2.5, 1.8→2.8) and brightened
- State-transition glow nodes added (3-layer concentric circles with white highlight + tappable hit target)
- Return-after-gap bloom upgraded to 3-layer concentric glow
- Growing tip ellipse enlarged (rx 42→60, ry 32→44) and brightened (opacity 0.6→0.75)
- SVG minimum height increased to 400px
- isStateTransition field added to Segment interface

## P2.NEW — Mobile 3D Trajectory Spectrograph (Aug 7 2026)

> **The "wow" feature for end users.** Full rotating 3D trajectory on the phone app — NOT a flat 2D line.
> Uses expo-gl + expo-three for native GPU rendering. Lightweight geometry (single BufferGeometry line ~500 vertices + head sprite + event markers). Capped at 30fps. Adaptive quality for older devices.

- [ ] **P2.NEW-1 — expo-gl + expo-three integration**
  - Install `expo-gl` and `three` (already a dep via expo-three)
  - Create `components/TrajectoryView3D.tsx` — native GL surface with Three.js scene
  - OrbitControls mapped to touch (pinch zoom, two-finger orbit, single-finger pan)
  - Frame budget: 30fps cap, lazy render (only re-render on data update or user interaction)
  - Memory target: <20MB GPU
  - Effort: 4-6 hours

- [ ] **P2.NEW-2 — Animated trajectory generation (birdsong-style)**
  - Trajectory draws itself in real-time as data arrives
  - Glowing head sprite traces the path like a pen on paper
  - Trail fades behind (older points dimmer, newer points brighter)
  - Auto-play on screen open (starts from beginning of current time window)
  - Speed controls: 0.5x, 1x, 2x, 5x, 10x
  - Pause/resume/scrub
  - Effort: 3-4 hours

- [ ] **P2.NEW-3 — Roots + Trajectory dual view on Roots screen**
  - Split or overlay: root system growing on left, trajectory drawing on right
  - SYNCHRONIZED: as trajectory draws a tight loop → root grows thick branch in that direction
  - As trajectory opens up → new shoots appear on root system
  - Same time axis, same data source, two metaphors
  - Passes 3 questions: useful (dual metaphor), honest (same data), beautiful (bioluminescent roots + glowing trail)
  - Effort: 6-8 hours (depends on root animation complexity)

- [ ] **P2.NEW-4 — Configurable axes on mobile**
  - Simplified axis picker (X/Y only, Z always = time on mobile for clarity)
  - Options: HRV, EDA, Movement, Temperature
  - Tap to swap axes
  - Effort: 1-2 hours

- [ ] **P2.NEW-5 — Tap point → context card**
  - Tap any point on trajectory → bottom sheet with:
    - Timestamp, state, confidence
    - Sensor values at that moment
    - Nearest check-in or log event
    - "What was happening?" context
  - Effort: 2-3 hours

  - Each population has reference band overlay on circadian chart

## BUILD 28 — VALIDATION-READY DATA COLLECTION (Added 2026-08-09)

> **Principle:** Every user is simultaneously a product user AND a research participant. Design data collection so it tests hypotheses from day one.
> **Reference:** `docs/HUGR_Validation_Study_Design_2026_08_09.md`
> **Prerequisite:** Samsung SDK_POLICY_ERROR resolved (Watch8 EDA flowing)

### Core Validation Features

- [ ] **B28.1 — "Is This Right?" confirmation flow**
  - Prompt after every state transition estimate (within 5 min)
  - User confirms or corrects state
  - Captures: app_estimate, user_confirmation, user_corrected_state, timestamps
  - Tests hypotheses H1, H2, H3, H7
  - Effort: 3-4 hours

- [ ] **B28.2 — Individual sensor scores stored alongside fusion**
  - Store what EDA-only, HRV-only, movement-only would estimate
  - Compare fusion accuracy vs individual sensor accuracy
  - Tests hypothesis H2 (multi-sensor > single sensor)
  - Effort: 2-3 hours

- [ ] **B28.3 — "When did you first notice?" retrospective timestamp**
  - Optional field on state confirmation: "When did you first notice this?"
  - Compare app detection time vs user awareness time
  - Tests hypothesis H3 (detection before awareness)
  - Effort: 1-2 hours

- [ ] **B28.4 — Event logger (medication, therapy, episodes)**
  - Simple "Log Event" button always accessible
  - Categories: medication, therapy, panic, craving, conflict, exercise, sleep disruption, other
  - Timestamp + category + optional free text + intensity (1-5)
  - Unlocks pharmacodynamic analysis (Patent Cluster 28)
  - Effort: 2-3 hours

- [ ] **B28.5 — Haptic A/B randomisation**
  - Days randomised: 4 haptic / 3 control per week
  - Control days: app detects transitions but does NOT fire haptics (silent)
  - User single-blinded (not told which day is which)
  - Store: isControlDay flag, pre/post state at 5min and 15min
  - Tests hypothesis H6 (haptics improve regulation)
  - Effort: 3-4 hours

- [ ] **B28.6 — Symbiosis Score daily tracking**
  - Compute and store daily Symbiosis Score (agreement between app and user)
  - Track improvement over first 14 days
  - Tests hypothesis H7 (2-5 day alignment)
  - Effort: 1-2 hours

- [ ] **B28.7 — Scheduled check-in frequency management**
  - Week 1-2: 3-4/day (calibration period)
  - Week 3+: 1-2/day (longitudinal)
  - Semi-random timing within windows (avoid habituation)
  - Effort: 2-3 hours

- [ ] **B28.8 — Research consent flow in onboarding**
  - Separate consent for: data collection, research use, anonymised sharing
  - GDPR-compliant data governance
  - Participant can withdraw at any time
  - Effort: 3-4 hours

## THREE-ENGINE DATA ACQUISITION STRATEGY (Added 2026-08-09)

> **Reference:** ChatGPT strategy session 2026-08-09. Core insight: don't recruit diagnosis-first, recruit hardware-first. Three independent engines feeding the same longitudinal physiology infrastructure.
> **North Star Metric:** Cumulative high-quality, appropriately consented longitudinal participant-days.
> **Secondary Metrics:** active participants | active studies | active external researchers | datasets | validated biomarkers | publications

### Engine 1 — Participant Acquisition (Hardware-First Global Recruitment)

- [ ] **E1.1 — Samsung Watch8 community outreach (Reddit, Facebook, Discord, Samsung Members)**
  - Hardware-first funnel: Own Watch8? → interested in physiology? → phenotype (clinical/control)
  - Target communities: r/GalaxyWatch, r/wearables, Samsung Members app, Facebook Galaxy Watch groups
  - Effort: 2-3 hours to draft posts + landing page

- [ ] **E1.2 — Prolific prescreening study ($100-200)**

### Evidence Architecture (How the engines combine)

```
Reddit/online cohort → scale (N=250+, 45,000 participant-days)
Paracelsus/private clinic → intensive multimodal clinical phenotyping (N=5-10, deep)
Formal clinical cohorts → replication (N=50-100)
Neuroimaging (NORMENT) → neural validation
Pharma studies → intervention/drug-response trajectories
```

Each dataset makes the others more interpretable.

## CLOSED-LOOP APP REDESIGN (Added 2026-08-09)

> **Core insight:** HUGR is NOT a tracker. It's a closed-loop physiological perturbation system.
> The tap is not a notification — it's a personalized, state-dependent, continuously-optimizing intervention.
> Two learning systems run simultaneously: (1) State prediction getting earlier, (2) Haptic optimization learning what works.
> **Reference:** `HUGR_Patent_Addendum_v8_Closed_Loop_Perturbation_2026_08_09.md`

### Haptics Tab Evolution (NOT a library — a learning system)

- [ ] **CL.1 — Replace haptic library with adaptive calibration flow**
  - Week 1: System delivers varied patterns, MEASURES EDA/HRV response to each
  - Week 2+: Shows "Your signal" — what the system has learned works for YOU
  - Advanced/curious users: can see response map, parameter space, optimization progress
  - Simple "How did that feel?" 1-tap feedback (helped / didn't help / didn't notice)
  - NOT a candy shop. System handles selection.
  - Effort: 4-6 hours

- [ ] **CL.2 — Adaptive breathing from measured respiratory rate**
  - Watch measures current respiratory rate from accelerometer/PPG
  - Breathing exercise STARTS at user's actual rate (not fixed 4-6 seconds)
  - Gradually slows toward target (e.g., 6 breaths/min)
  - Haptic guides rhythm (vibrate on inhale, pause on exhale)
  - Measure: did EDA drop? Did HRV improve? How much?
  - Next session: adapts curve based on measured response
  - Effort: 4-5 hours

- [ ] **CL.3 — Multiple perturbation strategies (not just breathing)**
  - Respiratory entrainment (slow the breath)
  - Heartbeat-coupled (sync to cardiac rhythm)
  - Coordinated reset (DISRUPT pathological patterns — rumination, craving loops)
  - Orienting response (snap attention — for dissociation)
  - Progressive deceleration (gradually calm without conscious effort)
  - Stochastic (unpredictable timing to prevent habituation)
  - System selects strategy based on detected state + learned response profile
  - Effort: 6-8 hours

- [ ] **CL.4 — Bayesian haptic parameter optimization**
  - Parameter space: carrier frequency × envelope shape × amplitude × duration × timing
  - Each haptic delivery = one observation (EDA response measured)
  - Bayesian optimization: efficient search through parameter space
  - Converges to individual optimum in 10-20 trials (not hundreds)
  - Warm-start from phenotype clusters (similar users → similar starting points)
  - Effort: 8-10 hours (algorithm design + implementation)

- [ ] **CL.5 — Autonomous haptic delivery (system-initiated)**
  - System detects pre-transition → delivers optimized haptic WITHOUT user opening app
  - Measures response → updates model
  - User only sees: "Your system noticed a shift and responded. Did it help?"
  - Background service on watch (already partially built in HapticService.kt)
  - Effort: 4-5 hours

- [ ] **CL.6 — Conditioned response tracking**
  - Measure: does the same haptic produce FASTER EDA response over time?
  - Track: response latency, response magnitude, response duration
  - If conditioning is working: latency decreases, magnitude increases, duration extends
  - This IS the evidence that the tap becomes a learned interoceptive anchor
  - Effort: 2-3 hours

### What STAYS unchanged:
- Home screen (state display, bioparticles) ✅
- Check-ins (ground truth for calibration) ✅
- Roots (pattern growth over time) ✅
- Clinician tab (real data for therapist) ✅
- Settings/onboarding ✅
- Simple/Full mode toggle ✅
- Spectrograph (sugar for curious users) ✅
- Logs, circadian, dimensional views ✅

## RESEARCH MODE — MULTI-PATIENT ANALYSIS (Added 2026-08-09)

> **Core insight:** Analyzing one patient is useful. Analyzing patterns ACROSS patients is where biomarkers are discovered.
> The clinician page needs a Research Mode that overlays multiple trajectories, finds clusters, and generates hypotheses.
> Compute is trivial (N=50 × 1000 points = 50K data points = milliseconds to process).
> Ethics: pseudonymised, consented, aggregate views only.

### Architecture

- [ ] **RM.1 — Multi-patient trajectory data model**
  - Extend DB schema: patient_trajectories table (patient_uuid, timestamp, eda, hrv, accel, state, confidence)
  - Cohort table: cohort_id, name, inclusion_criteria, created_by
  - Patient-cohort membership table
  - Effort: 2-3 hours

- [ ] **RM.2 — Research Mode toggle on Clinic page**
  - Individual Mode (current): one patient's data
  - Research Mode: multiple patients overlaid in same phase-space
  - Toggle in top bar or sidebar
  - Effort: 2-3 hours

- [ ] **RM.3 — Multi-trajectory overlay in spectrograph**
  - Each patient = different color trail in same 3D space
  - Opacity/thickness based on recency or confidence
  - Click any trail → identify which patient
  - Effort: 4-5 hours

- [ ] **RM.4 — Clustering algorithm (k-means / DBSCAN on trajectory features)**
  - Compute trajectory features: spread, velocity, cluster count, transition frequency, dominant state
  - Run clustering → identify phenotype groups
  - Display: color-code patients by cluster membership
  - "Patients in Cluster A all have high EDA variance + low HRV recovery"
  - Effort: 4-5 hours

- [ ] **RM.5 — Biomarker candidate discovery**
  - For each cluster: what distinguishes it from others?
  - Feature importance ranking (which sensor/metric best separates clusters)
  - Generate hypothesis: "EDA variance >X combined with HRV <Y predicts transition with Z% accuracy"
  - Display as ranked list of candidate biomarkers with confidence
  - Effort: 4-5 hours

- [ ] **RM.6 — Cohort comparison (drug vs placebo, pre vs post)**
  - Split cohort by any variable (medication, diagnosis, intervention, time period)
  - Overlay group trajectories with confidence bands
  - Statistical comparison: trajectory spread, velocity, state distribution
  - Export: publication-ready comparison figures
  - Effort: 4-5 hours

- [ ] **RM.7 — Pattern similarity alerts**
  - "Patient A's trajectory this week resembles Patient B's trajectory 3 weeks ago (before relapse)"
  - Cross-patient pattern matching using trajectory shape similarity (DTW or Fréchet distance)
  - Clinician alert: "This pattern has been associated with [outcome] in [N] similar patients"
  - Effort: 6-8 hours

## PARKED IDEAS — Document Now, Build Later

### HUGR Research Academy (Phase 3 — 12-18 months out)
> **Captured 2026-08-11.** Idea came during Innovation Norway application process. PARKED — not for current phase.

**Concept:** Personalised research literacy platform using the same mycelial/network interface as the clinician dashboard. Maps knowledge-space the way HUGR maps physiological state-space. From "what is a p-value" to "designing your own RCT." Interactive, adaptive, beautiful, social.

**Architecture:** Already exists — the 3D network on nerva.health IS a knowledge graph interface. Nodes = concepts/skills. Links = prerequisites. Trajectory = learning journey. Same codebase, different data.

**Levels:**
- Student: Learn research methods, stats, how to read papers, experiment design
- Researcher: Plan experiments, model hypotheses, discover biomarkers, write papers
- Clinician: Understand evidence, interpret results, apply to practice
- Pharma: Trial design, endpoint selection, regulatory pathway planning

**Revenue model:**
- Individual subscriptions (Duolingo model)
- University/department licensing (B2B)
- Pharma training contracts
- Integrated with HUGR Research Commons (learn → research → publish pipeline)

**Novel angle:** Student's own Watch8 physiological data informs learning pace (stress → slow down, flow state → push harder). Closed-loop learning optimisation.

**Why it feeds core business:**
- Students learn on platform → become researchers using Research Commons → generate longitudinal data
- Recruitment funnel disguised as education
- Differentiates HUGR from pure infrastructure providers

**Why NOT now:**
- Solo founder, no revenue, EDA not flowing
- OS1 must validate ONE thing (physiological intelligence)
- Build AFTER Research Commons has real users who show you what they struggle with
- Phase 3 (12-18 months) when resources and evidence exist

**Patent potential:** Adaptive personalised research competence mapping using network-based knowledge representation with physiological data integration (learning pace informed by real-time autonomic state). Add to next patent addendum.

## Signal Processing Pipeline (SP) — Research-Grade Data Quality
**Priority: HIGH — Must be in Build 29 (before any validation data is collected)**
**Rationale: The spectrograph, biomarker discovery, pharma endpoints, and fMRI correlation ALL depend on clean, validated signal processing. Without this, we have pretty pictures built on noise.**

### SP.1 — EDA Signal Processing
- [x] SP.1.1: Bandpass filter (0.05–5 Hz) for tonic/phasic decomposition
- [x] SP.1.2: Tonic SCL extraction (slow drift = baseline arousal)
- [x] SP.1.3: Phasic SCR extraction (fast peaks = event responses)
- [x] SP.1.4: Motion artifact detection (flag EDA segments where accelerometer shows movement > threshold)
- [x] SP.1.5: Skin contact quality indicator (detect when watch lifts off skin — EDA drops to 0 or goes erratic)
- [ ] SP.1.6: Adaptive baseline correction (rolling 60s window)

### SP.2 — HRV Signal Processing (from IBI)
- [x] SP.2.1: IBI artifact rejection (remove physiologically impossible intervals: <300ms or >2000ms)
- [x] SP.2.2: Ectopic beat detection and interpolation
- [x] SP.2.3: Time-domain HRV: RMSSD, SDNN, pNN50, mean HR
- [ ] SP.2.4: Frequency-domain HRV: LF (0.04–0.15 Hz), HF (0.15–0.4 Hz), LF/HF ratio (via Lomb-Scargle periodogram — handles uneven IBI spacing)
- [ ] SP.2.5: Non-linear HRV: Sample Entropy, DFA α1 (short-term fractal scaling)
- [x] SP.2.6: Respiratory rate estimation from HRV (RSA extraction)

### SP.3 — Accelerometer Processing
- [x] SP.3.1: Activity classification (still, walking, running, gesturing)
- [ ] SP.3.2: Motion intensity quantification (for EDA/PPG artifact flagging)
- [ ] SP.3.3: Sleep/wake detection (for circadian analysis)
- [x] SP.3.4: Tremor detection (relevant for medication side effects, anxiety)

### SP.4 — Multi-Modal Fusion (Research-Grade)
- [x] SP.4.1: Synchronised timestamping across all channels (EDA, IBI, Accel) — unified epoch clock
- [x] SP.4.2: Per-epoch data quality score (0–1) based on: skin contact, motion artifacts, signal-to-noise ratio
- [ ] SP.4.3: Confidence-weighted state estimation (low-quality epochs reduce confidence, don't corrupt estimate)
- [x] SP.4.4: Raw data storage at full resolution (25 Hz EDA, beat-by-beat IBI, 50 Hz accel) alongside processed features
- [ ] SP.4.5: Export format: HDF5 or Parquet with metadata (for researchers to re-analyse with their own pipelines)

### SP.5 — Validation Against Ground Truth
- [ ] SP.5.1: Compare HUGR HRV metrics against Polar H10 chest strap (gold standard for ambulatory HRV)
- [ ] SP.5.2: Compare HUGR EDA against Shimmer3 GSR+ (lab-grade EDA)
- [ ] SP.5.3: Document measurement agreement (Bland-Altman plots, ICC)
- [ ] SP.5.4: Publish validation data as supplementary material for first paper

**Note: Without SP.1–SP.4, the spectrograph is visualising noise. With them, it's visualising physiology. This is the difference between a toy and a research instrument.**

## Vibrotactile Median Nerve Modulation (VNM) — Research Hypotheses & Tasks
**Priority: HIGH — Informs haptic calibration design (CL.1-4) and validation study**
**Scientific basis: nMNS literature (2020-2024), ambienBeat (ACM 2020, 93 citations)**

### Research Hypotheses (for pilot/validation study):

**H1 (Interoceptive Awareness):** Participants receiving HUGR haptic signals during state transitions will demonstrate improved state-recognition accuracy (measured by "Is This Right?" confirmation rate) compared to control periods without haptics.

**H2 (Acute Vagal Modulation):** Sustained rhythmic vibrotactile stimulation (100-200 Hz, 5-30s duration) delivered to the volar wrist during elevated sympathetic states will produce measurable increases in RMSSD within 60 seconds of stimulus onset.

**H3 (Window of Tolerance Expansion):** Over 14 days of closed-loop haptic intervention, participants will show: (a) increased time spent within their personalised Window of Tolerance, (b) smoother state transitions (reduced trajectory "jump" magnitude), (c) faster return to baseline after perturbation.

**H4 (Autonomic Flexibility vs Parasympathetic Dominance):** The optimisation target is NOT maximised HRV or minimised EDA. It is increased VARIABILITY of state transitions (more states visited, smoother transitions between them). Rigid calm is as pathological as rigid activation.

**H5 (Device Independence):** After 30 days of use, participants will show improved autonomous state-recognition (measured by unprompted check-in accuracy) even during periods when haptics are withheld — suggesting conditioned interoceptive learning, not device dependence.

**H6 (Substance Disambiguation):** In participants who log substance use (caffeine, nicotine, prescribed stimulants, alcohol), the system will learn to differentiate substance-induced physiological signatures from emotional arousal, improving state estimation accuracy over time.

**H7 (Inside-Wrist Superiority):** Participants wearing the watch on the volar (inside) wrist will show: (a) higher EDA signal quality (fewer artifacts, better skin contact), (b) stronger haptic perception (lower detection threshold), (c) larger acute HRV response to vibrotactile stimulation, compared to dorsal (outside) wrist wear.

### Tasks:

- [ ] VNM.1: Document wearing guide (inside wrist recommended, positioning, strap tightness)
- [ ] VNM.2: Implement haptic frequency sweep test (50-250 Hz) in calibration flow — find individual resonant frequency
- [ ] VNM.3: Implement haptic amplitude threshold test — find minimum perceptible vibration per user
- [ ] VNM.4: Design three haptic modes: (a) recognition tap (brief, 50ms), (b) modulation rhythm (sustained, 5-30s, respiratory-coupled), (c) training pattern (paired with breathing exercise)
- [ ] VNM.5: Add inside/outside wrist toggle to app settings (affects signal processing parameters)
- [x] VNM.6: ECG_ON_DEMAND APPROVED by Samsung (2026-08-19) — QT monitoring now possible
- [x] VNM.7: Implement off-wrist detection (EDA → 0 + accel flat → flag as removed, don't interpolate)
- [x] VNM.8: Implement exercise vs panic disambiguation (high HR + high EDA + rhythmic accel = exercise; high HR + high EDA + still = emotional)
- [x] VNM.9: Implement re-baseline period after watch-on (first 2-5 min flagged as "settling")
- [ ] VNM.10: Literature review — optimal nMNS parameters for vagal modulation (frequency, amplitude, duration, duty cycle)

### North Star Update:
The HUGR system is not a tracker. It is not a vagal stimulator. It is a **closed-loop autonomic flexibility training system** that uses the median nerve pathway as both sensor conduit (EDA from skin) and intervention conduit (vibrotactile → NTS → vagal modulation). The goal is not calm — it is plasticity. The measure of success is not "time in green zone" — it is "smoothness and range of state transitions" and ultimately "device independence" (the user no longer needs HUGR because their intrinsic interoception has improved).

## ONBOARDING — Watch Battery Optimization (OB.1-3)
*Added: 2026-08-13 | Priority: HIGH (affects data continuity)*
*Status: Required for all deployments — without this, data stops when watch screen goes dark*

### The Problem
Android's "Sett appaktivitet på pause hvis ubrukt" (battery optimization) kills the HUGR foreground service when the watch screen turns off. This breaks continuous monitoring.

### Required Settings (must be part of onboarding):
1. Watch: Settings → Apps → HUGR → "Sett appaktivitet på pause hvis ubrukt" → OFF
2. Watch: Settings → Battery → Background usage limits → Never sleeping apps → Add HUGR
3. Watch: Settings → Battery → Battery optimization → HUGR → Don't optimize

### Tasks:
- [ ] OB.1: Wear OS onboarding screen — "For continuous monitoring, please disable battery optimization" with direct intent to settings
- [ ] OB.2: Detect if battery optimization is enabled and show persistent warning if data gaps detected
- [ ] OB.3: Phone app onboarding — guide user through watch settings with screenshots/animations
- [ ] OB.4: Document required settings in participant guide for Reddit recruitment
- [ ] OB.5: Test data continuity over 8+ hours with optimization disabled (confirm wake lock + foreground service survives)

### Notes:
- This is a known issue with ALL continuous health monitoring apps on Wear OS
- Samsung Health itself requires the same exemptions
- Must be communicated clearly to participants — data quality depends on this
- Consider adding a "data gap detector" that alerts if >5 minutes of missing data

## ADAPTIVE POWER MANAGEMENT (APM.1-12)
*Added: 2026-08-14 | Priority: HIGH (Build 30) | Depends on: BLE persistence (Build 29)*
*Research: docs/HUGR_Battery_Compliance_Accuracy_Strategy_2026_08_14.md*
*Patent: Cluster 38 (Context-Aware Adaptive Power Management)*

### The Problem
Galaxy Watch 8 with all sensors at full rate = ~12-16h battery. Research requires 24/7 including sleep. Solution: 4 adaptive power modes + smart charging window.

### Implementation (Build 30):
- [ ] APM.1: Watch — Implement 4 power modes (Full/Active/Rest/Sleep) with automatic context-based switching
- [ ] APM.2: Watch — Sleep detection algorithm (accelerometer stillness >15 min + HR in sleep range → enter Sleep mode)
- [ ] APM.3: Watch — State-transition escalation (fusion engine confidence >0.7 → escalate to Full mode for 2-5 min)
- [ ] APM.4: Watch — Ring buffer stores ALL raw data at full resolution regardless of current power mode (for later sync)
- [ ] APM.5: Phone — Batch sync handler (process 30-60s data chunks instead of per-sample BLE notifications)
- [ ] APM.6: Phone — Gap handler (mark charging gaps, interpolate tonic EDA, flag HRV as unavailable, compute quality scores)
- [ ] APM.7: App — Charging protocol onboarding (choose morning/evening/split, reminder to put watch back on)
- [ ] APM.8: App — Compliance dashboard (wear-time hours/day, streak counter, data quality badge)

### Compliance Tiers (Build 31+):
- [ ] APM.9: Consumer tier — gamification, streak counter, "even 16h/day is great" messaging
- [ ] APM.10: Research tier — prescribed charging window, weekly compliance report, automated check-in if <18h/day
- [ ] APM.11: Clinical tier — clinician-visible compliance on dashboard, alerts if >4h gap
- [ ] APM.12: Pharma tier — SDTM-compatible wear-time endpoint, protocol deviation logging, reason codes

### Notes:
- Build 29 (current): Full-blast mode is fine for N=1 validation. Neil charges during morning routine.
- Build 30: Implement APM.1-8 (adaptive modes + ring buffer + batch sync)
- Build 31+: Implement APM.9-12 (compliance tiers for multi-user deployment)
- SmartAPM research shows 36% battery extension with context-aware management
- Empatica achieves 48h with optimised sampling — our target is 24h+ with adaptive modes
- 30-45 min daily charging window is STANDARD in all continuous monitoring studies

## BLE CONNECTION PERSISTENCE (BLE.1-6)
*Added: 2026-08-13 | Priority: CRITICAL — nothing works without this*
*Status: ROOT CAUSE FOUND AND FIXED IN CODE (2026-08-13) — awaiting EAS build*

### The Problem
When user switches away from the HUGR phone app (checks messages, opens another app, screen locks), Android kills the BLE connection. User must manually reconnect every time they return. This makes continuous monitoring impossible.

### Root Cause (diagnosed 2026-08-13):
Build 29 crashed because of THREE manifest/permission issues:
1. Wrong foreground service type: Plugin declared `health` but service needs `connectedDevice|health`
2. Missing permission: `FOREGROUND_SERVICE_CONNECTED_DEVICE` not declared
3. Missing runtime type in startForeground(): Android 14+ requires explicit type flag
4. BLE background flag disabled: `react-native-ble-plx` had `isBackgroundEnabled: false`
See: `docs/BLE_FOREGROUND_SERVICE_CRASH_DIAGNOSIS_2026_08_13.md`

### Tasks:
- [x] BLE.1: Foreground service code EXISTS + crash fix applied (commit pending)
- [ ] BLE.2: Auto-reconnect logic — if connection drops, retry scan every 10 seconds (exponential backoff: 10s, 20s, 40s, max 60s)
- [ ] BLE.3: Connection state persistence — save last connected device ID, auto-connect on app launch without user tapping "Connect"
- [ ] BLE.4: Background data buffering — if phone app UI is not visible, still receive and store BLE data in local database
- [ ] BLE.5: Connection health indicator — show "Connected (stable)" / "Reconnecting..." / "Lost (X min ago)" in persistent notification
- [x] BLE.6: Wake lock already in HUGRForegroundService.kt (PARTIAL_WAKE_LOCK, 12h)

### Architecture:
```
Phone App Launch → Start BLE Foreground Service → Auto-scan for known device →
Connect → Subscribe → Receive data (even when UI backgrounded) →
If disconnect detected → Auto-retry (exponential backoff) →
If reconnected → Resume data flow seamlessly
```

### Notes:
- This is the SAME pattern used by Polar Beat, Garmin Connect, Whoop — all use foreground services
- React Native: use react-native-background-actions or headless JS task
- Must survive: app backgrounded, screen off, other apps in foreground, phone locked
- Battery impact: minimal (BLE is low energy by design, ~1-2% per hour)
- This is BUILD 29 PRIORITY 1 — before signal processing, before validation features

## TIERED INTELLIGENCE ARCHITECTURE (TIA.1-8)

*Added: 2026-08-13 | Priority: ARCHITECTURE — informs all future builds*
*Status: DESIGNED — see docs/HUGR_HYBRID_ARCHITECTURE_RESEARCH_2026_08_13.md*
*Patent: Clusters 36-37 filed conceptually*

### CRITICAL FINDING (2026-08-17):
Samsung SDK DOES collect data at full resolution (1 Hz EDA, 1 Hz HR/IBI, 25 Hz Accel) even when watch screen is off. Data is delivered in BATCHES, not lost. The sensor NEVER stops. Patent Cluster 39 filed for batch-aware architecture.

### CRITICAL FINDING UPDATE (2026-08-17 — confirmed via Samsung docs + StackOverflow):
- Samsung SDK `flush()` method forces immediate delivery of batched data WITHOUT affecting sensor sampling
- flush() does NOT introduce artifacts, does NOT change sampling rate, does NOT interrupt the sensor
- flush() resets the batch counter (next auto-push happens N samples AFTER flush, not from last auto-push)
- Battery impact: minimal when wake lock already held (our case)
- **ARCHITECTURE DECISION:** flush() every 30s + ring buffer + batch-aware BLE = COMPLETE solution
- flush() enables closed-loop (30s max latency), ring buffer ensures zero data loss, batch-aware BLE handles reconnection
- Neither alone is sufficient. BOTH are required for research-grade + closed-loop operation
- **IBI QUALITY CONCERN:** One developer reported "IBI readings collected with screen off weren't reliable" — must be validated during N=1 by comparing realtime vs batched IBI quality metrics

### SEAMLESS OPERATION REQUIREMENT (non-negotiable):
The system MUST function identically regardless of:
- Watch screen on/off/ambient (Samsung SDK handles this — data collected always)
- HUGR Wear OS app visible/background/not visible (foreground service keeps sensors running)
- Phone app foreground/background/killed (native foreground service maintains BLE)
- BLE connected/disconnected (ring buffer preserves data, sync on reconnect)
- NO crashes permitted on any device in any state combination
- NO data loss permitted in ANY combination of device states
- Research-grade accuracy maintained regardless of delivery mode (real-time vs batched)

### North Star:
The system operates at THREE confidence tiers. The loop NEVER fully stops — it degrades gracefully.
- Tier A (Watch autonomous): Simplified state estimation + conservative haptic with last-known parameters
- Tier B (Phone connected): Full Bayesian optimisation + precision haptic + real-time learning
- Tier C (Cloud/Clinician): Offline multi-patient analysis + phenotype discovery

### Tasks:
- [ ] TIA.1: Implement CompanionDeviceManager pairing on phone (OS-managed reconnection, REQUEST_COMPANION_RUN_IN_BACKGROUND)
- [ ] TIA.2: GATT keepalive ping every 20 seconds from phone (prevents Wear OS 30-second idle timeout)
- [ ] TIA.2a: flush() timer on watch — call edaTracker.flush() + heartRateTracker.flush() every 30 seconds (Samsung-documented mechanism for timely screen-off data delivery)
- [ ] TIA.2b: Validate flush() does not degrade IBI quality — compare realtime vs batched IBI during N=1 (log delivery_mode per data point)
- [ ] TIA.3: Watch local ring buffer — store 2-4 hours raw EDA/IBI/Accel at full resolution
- [ ] TIA.3a: Ring buffer handles Samsung SDK batched delivery — store ALL points with ORIGINAL timestamps from batch
- [ ] TIA.3b: Separate read/write pointers (local storage vs BLE transmission) — never overwrite unsynced data
- [ ] TIA.3c: Sync protocol — on BLE reconnect, transfer ALL unsynced data with sequence numbers and acknowledgement
- [ ] TIA.4: Watch simplified state estimator — threshold-based (EDA slope + HR delta + motion), runs locally
- [ ] TIA.5: Parameter sync protocol — phone sends Bayesian posterior parameters to watch via BLE characteristic
- [ ] TIA.6: Watch autonomous haptic delivery — conservative, high-confidence transitions only, using last-known parameters
- [ ] TIA.7: Reconnection evaluation — phone downloads watch buffer + decision log, compares against full model
- [ ] TIA.8: Confidence-aware UI — "Full precision" / "Autonomous mode" / "Disconnected" indicator on both watch and phone
- [ ] TIA.9: Dual-mode BLE transmission — real-time GATT notifications when connected + compressed batch sync when reconnecting
- [ ] TIA.10: Closed-loop continuity during batched periods — last-known state estimate with confidence decay, conservative interventions
- [ ] TIA.11: Phone temporal reconstruction — rebuild continuous 1 Hz time-series from batched arrivals using embedded timestamps
- [ ] TIA.12: Data quality flagging — per-epoch metadata: delivery_mode (realtime/batched), confidence_score, gap_duration, interpolation_applied

### Build Mapping:
- Build 29: BLE.1-6 (persistent connection) + TIA.1 + TIA.2
- Build 30: SP.1-5 (signal processing) + TIA.3 + TIA.4
- Build 31: TIA.5 + TIA.6 + TIA.7 + TIA.8

### Samsung Watch 8 — Hardware Moat:
ONLY consumer device with ALL FOUR requirements for closed-loop:
1. ✅ Continuous EDA (BioActive Sensor BIA electrodes)
2. ✅ Raw data SDK access (Samsung Health Sensor SDK — approved partnership)
3. ✅ IBI/HRV data (PPG continuous)
4. ✅ Programmable haptic motor (vibration motor, controllable via Kotlin)

No competitor can implement patent claim 35.1 on their hardware:
- Apple Watch: No EDA, no raw data access
- Fitbit: No raw SDK, spot-check EDA only
- Whoop: No EDA, no haptic
- Polar: No EDA, no haptic
- Empatica: No haptic motor (sensor only, ~€1500)

### Patent Addendum — New Clusters (2026-08-21)
- [ ] PA.43: Cluster 43 — Temporal Haptic Synthesis for Autonomic Intervention
  - Dynamically modulated inter-pulse intervals for perceptible temporal contours

### Key Research Findings (2026-08-21):
- HapticService was NEVER STARTED (root cause of no vibration in Builds 36w-37w)
- Fixed in Build 38w: direct vibrator call from BleGattService
- Samsung system patterns (Gallop, Heartbeat) use privileged haptic driver path — third-party API is weaker
- Psychophysics literature confirms: tactile temporal discrimination is BETTER than visual
- BuzzWear research: wrist-worn tactile languages achievable with high accuracy after training
- Inter-stimulus interval is major factor in pattern recognition (2025 study)
- "The message lives in the relationship between vibrations, not inside each vibration"
- Temporal modulation = primary channel; amplitude = secondary (where hardware permits)
- Architecture is hardware-forward: richer vocabulary on better future hardware without redesign

### Watch60 egress-only GATT activation correction — 2026-09-23

**WATCH-SIDE SOURCE / TEST GREEN — NO BUILD OR DEVICE ACTION.** The prior Watch-only readiness gap is resolved in the disposable Watch59 lineage: after a successful explicit `ACTIVATE READ-ONLY EGRESS` location result only, `EvidenceEgressActivity` requests the existing `BleGattService` with an explicit egress-only action. That mode registers only the four existing dedicated egress characteristics under the same service UUID; it omits source-journal acquisition, sensor receivers, health/transport tickers, telemetry/source/haptic characteristics, ordinary connection bookkeeping and `MainActivity`/`HealthSensorService` startup. Failed or incomplete locations request no GATT service, and an active standard service cannot be silently repurposed as egress-only (or vice versa). New policy contracts passed `4 / 4`; focused egress group passed `24 / 24`; the complete Watch unit suite passed `176 / 176` with zero failures/errors/skips. Exact-range and exporter protected hashes remain exact. **No Watch60 build, install, Watch launch, BLE operation, completed-ID entry, frozen-root access, transfer, receipt or verifier occurred.** A Watch60 candidate build has no remaining Watch source/test blocker, but needs a separately authorized greater-version, same-package/signer candidate-build admission. Physical egress remains blocked separately by Phone48’s absence of an explicit egress-only connect/discovery lifecycle; ordinary telemetry connection is not an admitted workaround. See `docs/HUGR_WATCH60_EGRESS_ONLY_GATT_ACTIVATION_CORRECTION_2026_09_23.md`.



**NSTAIR WATCH65 + PHONE57 ORDINARY-RUNTIME CANDIDATE PAIR — ARTIFACT ADMISSION GREEN / NOT INSTALLED.** One new same-package/signer, greater-version pair is now admitted as a **governed execution-path ordinary-runtime candidate**, not as a claim of literal strict topology isolation: Watch65 (`com.hugr.wearos`, code `65`, `0e6f3e24…8f4243`, 12,791,848 bytes) is a successor to recorded installed Watch64; Phone57 (`health.hugr.app`, code `57`, `049d8ec7…3e2795`, 151,998,178 bytes) is a successor to recorded installed Phone56. Phone57 EAS build `6e15ed5d-7ef3-4656-929a-8996ef8f2604` finished through the existing `@neilhugr/hugr-mobile` managed route. APK ZIP/CRC/alignment/signature checks, predecessor ZIP entry-name sets, and manifests normalized for version fields are green; Watch regression is **194/194**, Phone contracts **19/19**, TypeScript is green, and Phone regression is **113/113**.
